Method: 核心方法是一个精确解析映射：将带拓扑θ角的U(1) LGT映射为带涌现线性Stark势的非局域动力学约束（kinetically constrained）费米子模型。无θ项时，该框架将非热动力学解释为接近可积自由费米子模型导致的预热化（prethermal）行为；θ非零时，涌现Stark势解释冻结动力学。作者进一步以数值模拟和未来实验提案支持该预言。

Result: 该框架统一解释了两种实验观测：无θ项时表现为接近可积模型的预热化；非零θ时表现为Stark势诱导的冻结动力学。证明该对应关系可因涌现守恒律导致次扩散输运，并预言这些现象出现在更广泛的一类模型中，数值模拟予以支持。

Conclusion: 该工作为低维U(1) LGT的慢动力学提供了统一视角，揭示了通过Rydberg实验探索次扩散分形子（fractonic）流体力学的新途径，并预言这些现象具有更广泛的适用性。

Abstract: The dynamics of gauge theories is of fundamental interest, with low-dimensional systems displaying a rich phenomenology beyond the rapid thermalization typical of QCD and non-Abelian theories. In particular, recent quantum simulation experiments have reported robust and unexpected signatures of slow relaxation in generic regimes and at large volumes in one-dimensional U(1) lattice gauge theories (LGTs), whose origin remains poorly understood. Here, we present a framework that provides a unified perspective on such slow dynamics and establishes a new route for exploring subdiffusive fractonic hydrodynamics in Rydberg experiments. The backbone of our theory is an exact analytical mapping from U(1) LGTs with a topological $θ$ angle to non-local kinetically constrained fermionic models with an emergent linear Stark potential. In the absence of the $θ$ term, this framework interprets the non-thermal dynamics reported in recent experiments as prethermal behavior arising from proximity to an integrable free-fermion model. For non-zero $θ$, the emergent Stark potential instead explains the freezing dynamics observed in other recent experiments. We argue that this correspondence could also lead to subdiffusive transport due to emergent conservation laws. We predict that these phenomena arise in a much broader class of models, and support this prediction with numerical simulations and proposals for future experiments.

</details>


### [92] [Thermodynamic efficiency of communication channels](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25934)
*Nahuel Freitas,Pedro E. Harunari,Massimiliano Esposito*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文界定了一类受共同物理约束的人工与自然通信信道，推导出热力学成本的界：每次信道使用的熵产生下界为输入-输出互信息，二者之比定义热力学效率。与以往能量约束信道不同，热力学成本不仅归于输入符号本身，也归于相邻符号间的跃迁，因此最大化效率偏好"极少切换"的有偏输入而非容量最优输入；在二元对称信道中该偏好通过自发破缺对称性的叉式分岔出现，细胞感知的最小模型也呈现同样现象。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人工与自然系统中的通信普遍受物理约束，以往对能量约束信道的研究主要把成本归于输入符号本身，忽略了符号间跃迁的代价。作者希望建立更普适的框架，刻画此类信道真正的热力学成本，并重新审视"效率最优"的输入设计问题。

Method: 界定一类广泛捕获人工与自然系统中常见物理约束的通信信道；推导其熵产生的下界，并证明每次信道使用的熵产生以输入-输出互信息为下界，进而以互信息与熵产生之比定义热力学效率。分析中显式建模相邻符号之间的跃迁成本，并以二元对称信道和细胞感知最小模型为实例进行求解与分岔分析。

Result: 得到熵产生下界为互信息的结论；发现热力学成本须同时归于输入符号及其相邻符号之间的跃迁。因此最大化热力学效率并不能由容量最优输入实现，而偏好有偏、极少切换的输入。在二元对称信道中，这一偏好通过叉式分岔自发破缺信道对称性而出现；细胞感知的最小模型表现出相同现象。

Conclusion: 热力学效率与信息容量是两种不同的优化目标，前者要求有偏且转换稀疏的输入策略。该框架统一了人工与自然通信信道中的物理约束，并表明跃迁成本是决定高效通信策略的关键因素，为理解细胞感知等信息处理系统的设计原理提供了热力学视角。

Abstract: We identify a broad class of communication channels that captures common physical constraints in both artificial and natural systems and derive bounds on their thermodynamic cost. We find that the entropy production per channel use is bounded from below by the input-output mutual information, and their ratio -mutual information divided by entropy production- defines the thermodynamic efficiency. Unlike previous studies of energy-constrained communication channels, our analysis shows that thermodynamic costs must be assigned not only to the input symbols themselves, but also to transitions between successive symbols. As a result, maximizing thermodynamic efficiency favors a biased input that switches only rarely, rather than the capacity-achieving input. For the binary symmetric channel, this preference emerges through a pitchfork bifurcation that spontaneously breaks the symmetry of the channel. A minimal model of cellular sensing exhibits the same phenomenon.

</details>


### [93] [Intersection Exponents of Simple Random Walks in Two and Three Dimensions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25968)
*Qiyuan Shi,Runsheng Liu,Xinyi Li,Ming Li,Youjin Deng*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文研究多个独立随机游走组之间互不相交概率的代数衰减指数（相交指数）ξ，在二维验证数值方法，在三维计算多种组态下的整数值与连续值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随机游走相交指数在二维有精确公式，但三维及更复杂多组组态缺乏通用精确表达式，需要数值方法提供可靠指数值以支撑解析研究。

Method: 先以二维已知精确公式检验数值方法；随后在三维计算两组（分别含k与m条随机游走）的相交指数ξ(k,m)，并将m推广为连续参数λ，同时考察三组代表性组态。

Result: 二维结果与已知精确公式一致；三维得到k=1,2、m=1,…,7的ξ(k,m)，以及k=1,2,3、0.25≤λ≤3的连续值ξ(k,λ)，三组组态亦有结果；远离最小矩阶时函数随λ增大且斜率递减，符合Brownian相交指数的严格凹性。

Conclusion: 所获得的整数与连续相交指数为不相交随机路径的解析研究提供了数值参考，并验证了数值方法的有效性。

Abstract: The probability that several independent random walks avoid one another decays algebraically with the number of steps $N\to\infty$ in two and three dimensions, $P_N\sim N^{-ξ/2}$, where the intersection exponent $ξ$ depends on the number of random walks. More generally, one may consider several groups of independent random walks, with intersections allowed within each group and forbidden between different groups. We first study intersection exponents in two dimensions, where our results agree with the known exact formulas and test the numerical approach. In three dimensions, where no general exact expression is known, we determine $ξ$ for a range of cases. For two groups containing $k$ and $m$ random walks, respectively, we obtain the exponents $ξ(k,m)$ for $k=1,2$ and $m=1,2,\ldots,7$. We further extend $m$ to a continuous parameter $λ$, allowing us to obtain numerical values for $ξ(k,λ)$ for $k=1,2,3$ and $0.25\leqλ\leq3$. Away from the smallest moment orders, these functions increase with $λ$ with decreasing slopes, as expected from the strict concavity of Brownian intersection exponents. We also investigate three-group configurations for several representative cases. The resulting integer and continuous exponents supply numerical values for analytical studies of non-intersecting random paths.

</details>


### [94] [Statistical mechanics of classical fractons on a line](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25999)
*Ylias Sadki,Abhishodh Prakash,S. L. Sondhi*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文研究一维经典 Machian fracton（守恒全局偶极矩、通过位置依赖的成对惯性核耦合动量差）在非紧支撑核情形下的平衡统计力学与遍历性，发现指数衰减核可给出广延自由能、均匀非局域核需 Kac 重标度，并通过广义 HMWC 论证与有限尺度标度表明平移破缺密度序参量在所有波矢下消失；作者构造非可逆事件链蒙特卡洛（ECMC）算法，在保持偶极矩与总动量的前提下采样位置-动量相空间，结果与哈密顿动力学长时间平均定量一致，平衡液态呈现短程团簇与强非高斯单粒子动量尾。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有研究表明紧支撑相互作用核会在 Gibbs 配分函数中产生发散，并破坏遍历性与对称性、形成带粒子团簇的非平衡稳态，从而规避 Hohenberg-Mermin-Wagner-Coleman 定理。本文旨在考察非紧支撑核这一互补情形，明确其遍历性、自由能的广延性以及对称破缺行为，从而系统理解非紧核区的平衡液态性质。

Method: 1) 对指数衰减核，利用图拉普拉斯（graph-Laplacian）与矩阵树（matrix-tree）界证明自由能广延性，从而论证统计力学描述的有效性；2) 对均匀非局域核，指出自由能超广延并要求 Kac 重标度；3) 采用广义 Hohenberg-Mermin-Wagner-Coleman 论证并结合有限尺度标度分析对称破缺密度序参量；4) 构造非可逆事件链蒙特卡洛（ECMC）算法，在采样耦合的位置-动量相空间时守恒偶极矩与总动量，并与哈密顿动力学长时间平均作定量比对。

Result: 指数衰减核给出广延自由能，支持存在有效的统计力学描述；均匀非局域核给出超广延自由能，需 Kac 重标度。广义 HMWC 论证与有限尺度标度表明，对称破缺的密度序参量在所有波矢下消失，从而熔化紧支撑核原有的长程平移破缺密度序。ECMC 采样与哈密顿动力学长时间平均定量吻合，平衡液态展现择优短程团簇与强非高斯单粒子动量尾，后者与位置-动量的强关联有关。

Conclusion: 本文系统刻画了非紧核区一维经典 Machian fracton 的平衡液态性质，确认其遍历性与统计力学自洽性（指数核广延、均匀核需 Kac 重标度），并证明长程平移破缺密度序在非紧核下被熔化；所提出的守恒偶极矩与总动量的 ECMC 算法可作为采样此类约束系统的有效工具。弛豫机制由姊妹论文另行研究。

Abstract: We study the equilibrium statistical mechanics of one-dimensional classical Machian fractons: particles whose dynamics conserves a global dipole moment and whose Hamiltonian couples momentum differences through a position-dependent pair-inertia kernel. Compactly supported interaction kernels have a divergence in the Gibbs partition function, and have been shown to break ergodicity and symmetry by forming non-equilibrium steady states with particle clusters, evading the Hohenberg-Mermin-Wagner-Coleman theorem. In this paper, we consider kernels with non-compact support and study their ergodic properties. For exponentially decaying kernels, graph-Laplacian and matrix-tree bounds provide an extensive free energy suggesting that a putative statistical mechanical description is valid. Similarly, uniform non-local kernels have a super-extensive free energy and require a Kac rescaling. A generalized Hohenberg--Mermin--Wagner--Coleman argument, supported by finite-size scaling, implies symmetry-breaking density order parameter vanishes at all wave vectors melting the long-range translation-breaking density order of compact kernels. To study the resulting equilibrium ensemble, we construct a nonreversible event-chain Monte Carlo (ECMC) algorithm that samples the coupled position-momentum phase space while preserving the dipole moment and total momentum. The ECMC sampling is shown to quantitatively match long time-averaged quantites in Hamiltonian dynamics. The equilibrium liquid exhibits preferred short-range clustering and strongly non-Gaussian single-particle momentum tails associated with the correlated nature of positions and momenta. This paper provides a detailed investigation into the equilibrium liquid properties of the non-compact regime, whilst the companion paper investigates the mechanisms that relax the liquid.

</details>


### [95] [Counterions between walls with surface charge modulations: Exact Poisson-Boltzmann solutions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26356)
*Ladislav Šamaj*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 在Poisson-Boltzmann极限下，研究位置依赖的表面电荷调制对两平行壁间有效压力（力）的影响。蒙特卡洛模拟预测电荷调制会降低压力；而基于二维Liouville方程精确解的解析结果表明，电荷调制在小间距d时增大压力、在大间距d时减小压力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究两平行带电壁间经典点电荷系统的热平衡，核心动机是揭示表面电荷调制（非均匀表面电荷分布）如何影响壁间有效压力，并与相同平均表面电荷密度的均匀带电壁情形进行对比。

Method: 限制表面电荷仅沿一个方向调制；利用二维Liouville方程的一般解构造静电势的精确解，并由该电势反推出壁面上的表面电荷密度；同时采用高温下的蒙特卡洛模拟。

Result: 蒙特卡洛模拟预测：与具有相同平均表面电荷密度的均匀带电壁面相比，表面电荷调制会降低壁间压力。精确解析结果则显示：表面电荷调制既可增大压力（小间距d），也可减小压力（大间距d）。

Conclusion: 由于表面电荷密度是由电势反推生成的，精确结果仅适用于具有特定表面电荷密度随距离d变化的有限模型集合；表面电荷调制对压力的影响依赖于壁间距，表现为小d时增压、大d时减压，与纯粹蒙特卡洛预测的单调降低趋势不完全一致。

Abstract: We study the thermal equilibrium of a classical system of identical point charges moving between two walls with parallel surfaces at a distance $d$, charged symmetrically with position-dependent surface charge densities. Specifically, we study the effect of surface charge modulation on the effective force (pressure) between the walls in the Poisson-Boltzmann limit. Based on Monte Carlo simulations at high temperatures, it is predicted that surface charge modulation reduces the pressure between the walls compared to uniformly charged wall surfaces with the same average surface charge density. We restrict ourselves to surface charge modulations in only one direction and, using a general solution of the two-dimensional Liouville equation, we construct exact solutions for the electrostatic potential. The surface charge density on the walls is generated inversely from this potential, which means that our exact results apply to a limited set of models with a specific variation of the surface charge density with distance $d$. Explicit analytical results show that surface charge modulation can both increase (small distances $d$) and decrease (large $d$) the pressure between the walls.

</details>


### [96] [A Statistical Analysis of Diffusion Dynamics in Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26759)
*A. Talebi*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文从网络拉普拉斯算子特征谱的统计性质出发，研究网络扩散的统计特性，并提出系统扩散性较弱的一个条件，分析网络结构变化对整体扩散动力学的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 以往网络扩散研究主要依赖拉普拉斯算子的次小特征值（代数连通度）来确定最慢衰减模式的时间尺度，需计算全部特征值，难以适用于大规模网络，且缺乏对特征谱统计性质的刻画。

Method: 考察网络拉普拉斯算子特征谱的统计性质，以此替代逐一计算全部特征值的方式，用于分析网络扩散的统计特性；并给出系统扩散性较弱（less diffusive）的条件，进一步研究网络结构变化如何影响整体扩散动力学。

Result: 该方法无需计算全部特征值即可刻画网络扩散的统计性质，因而适用于大规模网络；同时给出了系统扩散性较弱所满足的条件，并揭示了网络结构变化与整体扩散动力学之间的关系。

Conclusion: 通过拉普拉斯特征谱的统计性质研究网络扩散，可在大规模网络上高效分析扩散统计特性；所提出的弱扩散条件及结构-扩散关系为理解与调控网络扩散动力学提供了新的分析视角。

Abstract: Network diffusion is a well-known model for studying how a quantity in a network propagates to reach a steady state, where its value becomes uniformly distributed across all nodes. Previous studies of network diffusion have mainly focused on the second-smallest eigenvalue of the network Laplacian to determine the timescale associated with the slowest decaying mode. However, this study investigates the eigenspectrum of the network Laplacian in terms of its statistical properties. This method is useful for studying the statistical properties of network diffusion without calculating all eigenvalues, which can be useful for large networks. Additionally, this study introduces a condition under which the system is less diffusive and investigates how changes in the network structure influence the overall diffusion dynamics.

</details>

### [97] [Re-entrant p-wave superconductivity in a Chern Dartboard Insulator](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25205)
*Yueyang Wu,Rebecca Chan,Taylor L. Hughes,Philip W. Phillips*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 该文建立了含轨道间配对与能带Hatsugai-Kohmoto (HK) 相互作用的双带超导精确理论，应用于Chern dartboard绝缘体，发现HK相互作用可诱导超导及绝缘-超导-绝缘重入转变。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 先前BCS理论预言在较小配对强度g且化学势μ位于能隙内时系统处于绝缘相；作者希望探究强关联HK相互作用如何改变这一图像，是否能在原本的绝缘区诱导超导，并厘清HK相互作用与轨道间配对的协同效应。

Method: 针对双带超导体建立精确理论，同时包含轨道间配对与能带Hatsugai-Kohmoto (HK) 相互作用；将其应用于Chern dartboard绝缘体，解析计算配对磁化率与配对关联。

Result: 在BCS预言的绝缘相（小g、μ在能隙内）中，HK相互作用通过抬升双占据能带使其穿过化学势，从而诱导超导，并可能形成Chern dartboard超导体；在较大参数范围（U=1, μ=0.5）内出现绝缘-超导-绝缘重入转变。

Conclusion: 重入转变源于HK相互作用与轨道间配对之间的相互纠缠（conflation）效应。

Abstract: We formulate an exact theory for a 2-band superconductor with inter-orbital pairing and band Hatsugai-Kohmoto (HK) interactions. We apply this theory to a Chern dartboard insulator and compute the pair susceptibility and pair correlations analytically. We find that in the insulating phase previously predicted by BCS theory (small pairing strength $g$, chemical potential $μ$ in the gap), the HK interaction can induce superconductivity, and possibly a Chern dartboard superconductor, by lifting the doubly-occupied band and making it cross the chemical potential. From this HK-induced superconductor, we notice that in a significant parameter range (HK interaction strength $U = 1$, $μ= 0.5$), an insulator-superconductor-insulator re-entrant transition exists. We isolate the re-entrance to a conflation between HK interactions and inter-orbital pairings.

</details>


### [98] [Model Fractional Quantum Hall States on Lattices: Exact Parent Hamiltonians and Routes to Realization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25585)
*Guangyue Ji,Jinjie Zhang*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 该论文构建了格点分数量子霍尔模型的厄米母哈密顿量，使连续最低朗道能级模型态成为精确零模，并据此研究Laughlin态与Moore–Read态在格点及多带情形下的稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实验上已在ν=1/2实现玻色Laughlin态，非阿贝尔拓扑相也接近可实验探测；理论上亟需统一框架，将格点FQH模型态、精确母哈密顿量与实验可达相互作用联系起来。

Method: 系统构造厄米母哈密顿量，使采样到格点的连续LLL模型态为精确零模；以这些模型流形为定量基准，在投影格点LLL中研究短程密度相互作用与两体在位排斥，并进行完整多带计算。

Result: 在所研究通量密度下，短程密度相互作用稳定ν=1/3与1/4的Laughlin基态流形，两体在位排斥支持ν=1的Moore–Read三重态；多带计算显示Laughlin流形对带间混合稳健，而Moore–Read三重态谱隔离性下降并最终与竞争态发生能级交叉。

Conclusion: 该框架为利用格点模型态指导实验可及阿贝尔与非阿贝尔FQH态的探索提供了理论基础。

Abstract: Recent advances in engineered quantum platforms have enabled the realization of bosonic Laughlin states at $ν=1/2$ and brought non-Abelian topological phases within experimental reach. A central theoretical challenge is to develop a unified framework connecting lattice fractional quantum Hall (FQH) model states, exact parent Hamiltonians, and experimentally accessible interactions. We systematically construct Hermitian parent Hamiltonians for which continuum lowest Landau level (LLL) model states sampled on lattice sites are exact zero modes. Using these model manifolds as quantitative references, we find that, within the projected lattice LLL at the flux densities studied, short-range density interactions stabilize Laughlin ground-state manifolds at $ν=1/3$ and $1/4$, while two-body onsite repulsion supports a Moore--Read triplet at $ν=1$. Full multiband calculations reveal a sharp contrast: the Laughlin manifolds remain robust against interband mixing on the studied tori, whereas the Moore--Read triplet becomes less spectrally isolated and eventually undergoes a level crossing with competing states near the interband scale. Our framework provides a theoretical foundation for using lattice model states to guide the search for experimentally accessible Abelian and non-Abelian FQH states.

</details>


### [99] [Kitaev spin liquid in superconducting networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25210)
*Guilherme Delfino,Mehmet Dede,Dmitry Green,Michael J. Manfra,Charles M. Marcus,Claudio Chamon*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文提出在超导超材料中实现Kitaev蜂窝哈密顿量的方案：以库珀对盒编码自旋自由度，通过耗尽半导体-超导异质结构介导键向相互作用，无需自旋-轨道耦合；利用面外磁通与半导体费米能作为控制参数，并识别出实验可及的运行区间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Kitaev蜂窝模型是典型的量子自旋液体模型，但其在真实材料中的实现通常依赖自旋-轨道耦合等苛刻条件，实验实现困难。作者希望找到一条不依赖自旋-轨道耦合、且能在实验可及尺度上实现的替代路径，以推动平衡态量子自旋液体的工程化构造。

Method: 架构由库珀对盒通过耗尽半导体-超导异质结构耦合而成。库珀对盒编码有效自旋自由度，异质结构中的正常与反常虚传播过程介导键向相互作用。使用两个关键控制参数：面外磁通和半导体费米能。前者调控干涉并区分键方向，后者调至带底附近时产生涌现的Nambu交换对称性，从而强制所需的键方向性。通过数值计算确定工作区间。

Result: 研究表明，无需自旋-轨道耦合即可实现键向相互作用；面外磁通控制干涉与键方向区分，费米能靠近带底时涌现Nambu交换对称性以强制键方向性。数值计算识别出一个具有可控修正的工作区间，其相关能量与长度尺度均在实验可及范围内。

Conclusion: 该工作确立了在工程化超导网络中实现平衡态量子自旋液体的一条可行路线，为基于超导超材料的自旋液体模拟提供了新方案。

Abstract: We propose a realization of the Kitaev honeycomb Hamiltonian -- an archetypal spin-liquid model -- in a superconducting metamaterial. The architecture consists of Cooper-pair boxes coupled through depleted semiconductor--superconductor heterostructures that do not require spin--orbit coupling. The Cooper-pair boxes encode effective spin degrees of freedom, while normal and anomalous virtual propagation through the heterostructures mediate bond-directional interactions. Two key control parameters are an out-of-plane magnetic flux and the semiconductor Fermi energy. The former controls interference and distinguishes the bond directions, while tuning the latter close to the bottom of the band gives rise to an emergent Nambu-exchange symmetry that enforces the required bond directionality. Through numerical calculations, we identify an operating regime with controlled corrections, with associated energy and length scales within experimental reach. These results establish a route toward equilibrium quantum spin liquids in engineered superconducting networks.

</details>


### [100] [From Effective Temperature to Non-Boltzmann State Selection in Driven-Dissipative Quantum Criticality](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25242)
*Tiago Jorge,Jens Paaske,Pedro Ribeiro*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文研究电压偏置的电子Lipkin-Meshkov-Glick模型，通过大N处理揭示了过阻尼开放量子临界区，发现量子临界扇区中的涨落由有效温度Teff支配，强偏置下经三临界点转变为一阶相变；同一Teff虽统治强驱动区但随序参量空间变化，稳态由非玻尔兹曼规则选择。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非平衡量子临界稳态能否用有效温度描述仍是未解问题，作者希望在驱动-耗散集体自旋模型中对此进行检验。

Method: 采用受控的大N处理，研究电压偏置的电子Lipkin-Meshkov-Glick模型（集体自旋耦合金属电极的最小驱动-耗散模型），探讨由温度和电压组织的量子临界扇区。

Result: 发现一个区别于封闭模型的过阻尼开放量子临界区；量子临界扇区中的涨落由有效温度Teff支配；强偏置下通过三临界点转变为一阶相变；同一Teff也支配强驱动区，但随序参量空间变化，稳态由非玻尔兹曼规则选择，使一阶相变偏离确定性势的等深点。

Conclusion: 驱动临界性仍由有效温度组织，但同时揭示出非热态的稳态选择机制，表明有效温度描述在不平衡量子临界中具有适用性但需引入非玻尔兹曼选择规则。

Abstract: Whether a nonequilibrium quantum-critical steady state can be described by an effective temperature remains an open question. We address it in a voltage-biased electronic Lipkin--Meshkov--Glick model, a minimal driven-dissipative model of a collective spin coupled to metallic leads. A controlled large-$N$ treatment reveals an overdamped open quantum-critical regime distinct from the closed model. The surrounding quantum critical fan, organized by temperature and voltage, is explored and fluctuations are found to be governed by an effective temperature $T_{\rm eff}$. At strong bias, the transition becomes first-order through a tricritical point. Remarkably, the same $T_{\rm eff}$ governs the strongly driven regime, but now varies across the entire order-parameter landscape. The steady state is therefore selected by a non-Boltzmann rule, shifting the first-order transition away from the equal-depth point of a deterministic potential. Driven criticality thus remains organized by an effective temperature while revealing non-thermal state selection.

</details>


### [101] [Extended symmetric quantum phase in a honeycomb Heisenberg model with sublattice-selective interactions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25243)
*Nai Chao Hu,Xing-Yu Zhang,Yuchi He,Nick Bultinck*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 该论文通过iPEPS、圆柱DMRG与精确对角化研究蜂窝格子上仅对B子格施加次近邻交换J₂'的自旋1/2反铁磁Heisenberg模型，发现在0.4≲J₂'/J₁≲0.6存在无磁序、无价键序、无手性自旋序的扩展对称区间，并倾向于支持无能隙量子自旋液体基态。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 受二维双层体系启发，作者希望探究蜂窝格子上非均匀阻挫交换（仅B子格具有次近邻耦合）所诱导的基态性质，特别是是否存在超越常规磁序/价键序的量子自旋液体态，并厘清其是否无能隙及对称性保护机制。

Method: 采用无限投影纠缠对态（iPEPS）、圆柱上的变分均匀矩阵乘积态（MPS）以及精确对角化，对含J₁与仅B子格J₂'的蜂窝格子自旋1/2反铁磁Heisenberg模型进行数值计算；在J₂'/J₁=0.5处系统改变键维并做关联长度外推，同时分析各观测量随有限键维诱导关联长度的代数标度行为，并构造parton态进行分析。

Result: 数值结果显示在0.4≲J₂'/J₁≲0.6的扩展对称区间内，局域可观测量不呈现磁序、价键序或手性自旋序；在J₂'/J₁=0.5处，PEPS关联长度随键维系统性增长，逆关联长度外推趋向消失极限，且多观测量随有限键维关联长度呈代数标度，支持无能隙自旋液体基态；并提出受平移、时间反演与三重旋转对称性保护的Z₂ Dirac自旋液体parton态作为候选解释。

Conclusion: 作者认为，受平移、时间反演和三重旋转对称性保护的Z₂ Dirac自旋液体parton态是解释数值结果的有力候选态；同时也讨论了该对称基态为具有小能隙的无特征短程纠缠态（featureless SRE）的可能性，因而基态是否严格无能隙仍未完全定论。

Abstract: Motivated by two-dimensional bilayer systems we numerically study an anti-ferromagnetic spin-$1/2$ Heisenberg model on the honeycomb lattice with a nearest-neighbour exchange coupling $J_1$, and a next-nearest-neighbour exchange coupling $J_2'$ for the B sublattice sites only. Using infinite Projected Entangled-Pair States (iPEPS), variational uniform matrix-product states on cylinders, and exact diagonalization, we find an extended symmetric regime $0.4\lesssim J'_2/J_1\lesssim0.6$, in which local observables show no magnetic, valence-bond, or chiral spin order. At $J'_2/J_1=0.5$, the PEPS correlation length grows systematically with bond dimension, and an inverse-correlation-length extrapolation favors a vanishing limit. We also find that various observables scale algebraically with the finite bond-dimension-induced correlation length, which points to a gapless spin liquid ground state. We propose a $\mathbb{Z}_2$ Dirac spin liquid parton state, with Dirac points that are protected by translation, time-reversal and three-fold rotation symmetry, as a promising candidate state to explain the numerical results. We also discuss the possibility that the symmetric ground state is a featureless, short-range entangled state with a small gap.

</details>


### [102] [Thermally melted quadrupolar order and intrinsically quantum phases in 5$d^1$ double perovskites](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25283)
*Rong Cong,Ginevra Corsale,Ilija K. Nikolov,Wenjuan Zhang,Nandini Trivedi,Vesna F. Mitrović*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 该工作用大规模经典蒙特卡洛模拟研究FCC格子上投影j=3/2流形的d^1双钙钛矿，发现四子格反铁磁和铁磁相在经典框架下稳健，而共面倾斜FM[110]态完全失稳，是量子涨落驱动的内禀量子态。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自旋轨道耦合的d^1双钙钛矿具有自旋、轨道与四极自由度的复杂交织，量子平均场理论预测了众多奇异相，但这些相在热涨落下的稳定性以及在经典框架中能否复现仍不清楚，需要超越平均场来检验。

Method: 在FCC格子的投影j=3/2流形上进行大规模经典蒙特卡洛模拟，不预设磁对称性使复杂有序模式自发涌现，并进行系统的热力学绘图与经典-量子稳定性边界的隔离分析。

Result: 热涨落使中间温度的四极相在相图部分区域熔化，而在其他区域完整存活；四子格反铁磁与铁磁相在经典描述下稳健，共面倾斜FM[110]态则完全失稳。

Conclusion: 主要磁相在经典描述内是稳健的，系统核心物理由弱纠缠、短程关联的自由度刻画；FM[110]相被认定为量子涨落产生的内禀量子态，热涨落对不同磁性及四极有序相的稳定性起关键作用。

Abstract: Spin-orbit-coupled $d^1$ double perovskites exhibit a rich interplay of spin, orbital, and quadrupolar degrees of freedom (DOF), giving rise to competing magnetic and multipolar phases. Although quantum mean-field theories predict many exotic phases, their stability against thermal fluctuations and reproducibility within a classical framework remain an open question. Here, we surpass the mean-field limitations by deploying large-scale classical Monte Carlo simulations on the projected $j=3/2$ manifold of the FCC lattice, allowing complex ordering patterns to emerge spontaneously without preassigned magnetic symmetries. Our thermodynamic mapping reveals that thermal fluctuations melt the intermediate-temperature quadrupolar phase over part of the phase diagram, while it survives intact elsewhere. Crucially, by systematically isolating the boundary between classical and quantum stability, we demonstrate that while the four-sublattice antiferromagnetic and ferromagnetic (FM) phases are robustly classical, the coplanar canted FM[110] state completely destabilizes. This identifies the FM[110] phase as an intrinsically quantum state born out of quantum fluctuations. Our results demonstrate that the dominant magnetic phases are robust within a classical description, where the essential physics of the system is captured by weakly entangled, short-range correlated DOF and highlight the role of thermal fluctuations in determining the stability of different types of magnetic and quadrupolar order.

</details>


### [103] [Lattice in Line: Optimized DMRG ordering for complex lattice geometries](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25384)
*Roman Rausch*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文研究如何为一维DMRG方法中周期性2D/3D团簇的"格点编译"寻找最优线路枚举，通过分析图带宽B、割宽C和平均相互作用距离R三个代理损失函数，提出基于可满足性（SAT）与约束规划（CP-SAT）的分阶段优化方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 密度矩阵重整化群（DMRG）虽是一维张量网络技术，但也可用于周期性2D/3D团簇和分子，只需先将格点沿一条线枚举（"格点编译"）。枚举顺序的优劣直接影响DMRG精度，但经典启发式方法在小团簇上也不可靠，因此需要一个能给出高质量排序与严格界限的优化框架。

Method: 针对大量阻挫磁性相关团簇构建哈密顿量的矩阵乘积算符（MPO），分析三个代理损失函数：图带宽B（最大相互作用范围）、割宽C（跨越割的最大键数）和平均相互作用距离R。采用分阶段优化方法，基于布尔可满足性（SAT）与约束规划（CP）求解器，主要是Google OR-Tools的CP-SAT（CP传播与SAT子句学习的混合）。

Result: C决定SU(2)海森堡MPO键维数的峰值，R决定平均值。按字典序优化(C,R)可获得最佳能量。优化B间接降低C，但效率不如直接优化C；B本身基本无关紧要，因为好的DMRG能量可以对应较大的B。经典启发式（如Reverse Cuthill–McKee）即使在小团簇上也不可靠，QUBO表述仅能带来边际改进；而所提出的SAT/CP-SAT分阶段方法能显著改善排序并提供严格界限。

Conclusion: 割宽C和平均相互作用距离R是决定DMRG质量的关键指标，按(C,R)字典序优化最优；基于CP-SAT的分阶段优化方法优于经典启发式和QUBO方法，能提供更好的排序及严格界限。相应代码Lattice in Line已在GitHub开源。

Abstract: The density-matrix renormalization group (DMRG) is a one-dimensional tensor-network technique, but it is not limited to one-dimensional systems: it can be applied to periodic 2D and 3D clusters and molecules, provided their sites are first enumerated along a line; a step one may call "lattice compilation".
  This paper discusses three proxy loss functions for finding this optimal enumeration: the graph bandwidth $B$ (maximum interaction range), the cutwidth $C$ (maximum number of bonds crossing a cut), and the average interaction range $R$. Constructing the Hamiltonian MPO (matrix-product operator) for a large set of clusters that are of interest in frustrated magnetism, I find that $C$ determines the peak SU(2) Heisenberg MPO bond dimension and $R$ the average one. Targeting $(C,R)$ lexicographically yields the best energies. Targeting $B$ indirectly reduces $C$, but not as efficiently as targeting $C$ directly. Otherwise, the value of $B$ itself is largely irrelevant in the sense that good DMRG energies can have large $B$.
  To perform the optimization, classic heuristics (e.g. reverse Cuthill--McKee) prove unreliable even for small clusters, and I find that a QUBO formulation improves them only marginally. Instead I propose a staged optimization built on Boolean satisfiability (SAT) and constraint-programming (CP) solvers, chiefly CP-SAT of Google's OR-Tools, a hybrid of CP propagation and SAT clause learning. This approach yields significantly better orderings together with rigorous bounds.
  The corresponding Lattice in Line code is available at https://github.com/spinflip/lattice_in_line and was designed with extensive use of the Fable 5 large language model.

</details>


### [104] [Coherent lattice dynamics distinguish Holstein and Su-Schrieffer-Heeger electron-phonon coupling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25418)
*Debshikha Banerjee,Jinu Thomas,G. Alvarez,T. P. Devereaux,T. A. Maier,S. Johnston*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 通过泵浦-探测超快光谱结合含时密度矩阵重整化群模拟，比较了Hubbard-Holstein与Hubbard-SSH模型，发现两者电子响应相似但晶格动力学存在本质差异，相干晶格动力学可作为区分电子-声子耦合微观形式的非平衡探针。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 超快泵浦-探测光谱是研究强关联体系中电子-声子（e-ph）相互作用的强有力手段，但不同微观电子-声子耦合机制如何影响晶格的非平衡响应这一问题尚缺乏系统研究。

Method: 对泵浦驱动的一维Mott绝缘体开展含时密度矩阵重整化群（tDMRG）模拟，声子分别通过Holstein相互作用与Su-Schrieffer-Heeger（SSH）相互作用与电子耦合，并改变声子频率与泵浦参数以检验结果的稳健性。

Result: 在Mott绝缘区，两种模型的电子响应定性相似，均由Hubbard模型相同的激发与共振条件主导；但晶格动力学差异显著：当泵浦与裸声子频率共振时，Hubbard-Holstein模型表现出长寿命相干声子振荡，而Hubbard-SSH模型则无此振荡。这一对比特征对声子频率与泵浦参数的变化保持稳健。

Conclusion: 相干晶格动力学能够为电子-声子耦合的微观形式提供直接的非平衡探测手段。

Abstract: Ultrafast pump-probe spectroscopy provides a powerful means to investigate electron-phonon (e-ph) interactions in strongly correlated systems. Nevertheless, the question of how different microscopic e-ph coupling mechanisms influence the lattice's nonequilibrium response has not been widely addressed. We perform time-dependent density matrix renormalization group simulations on a pumped one-dimensional Mott insulator coupled to phonons through either Holstein or Su-Schrieffer-Heeger (SSH) interactions. The electronic responses of the two models are qualitatively similar in the Mott-insulating regime and are governed by the same excitations and resonance conditions of the Hubbard model. In contrast, the lattice dynamics of the two models differ substantially. While the Hubbard-Holstein model exhibits long-lived coherent phonon oscillations when the pump resonates with the bare phonon frequency, no such oscillations are observed for the Hubbard-SSH model. These contrasting responses remain robust against changes in phonon frequency and pump parameters, suggesting that coherent lattice dynamics can provide a direct nonequilibrium probe of the microscopic form of e-ph coupling.

</details>


### [105] [Efficiency of Continuous-Time Quantum Monte Carlo Updates in the Ferromagnetic State of the Doped SU(3) Fermi-Hubbard Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25494)
*Juntaro Fujii,Kazuki Yamamoto,Akihisa Koga*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文在动力学平均场理论框架下，研究掺杂SU(3)费米-哈伯德模型的连续时间杂化展开量子蒙特卡洛求解器采样效率，比较不同更新方案在铁磁与顺磁区的表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在低温强耦合及空穴掺杂偏离三分之一填充时会出现铁磁态，该区域采样效率低，需要寻找更优的蒙特卡洛更新方案以减少自相关时间。

Method: 采用基于分段的连续时间杂化展开量子蒙特卡洛求解器，在动力学平均场理论中模拟掺杂SU(3)费米-哈伯德模型，并对比基础局域更新与加入双翻转更新、味置换更新等方案。

Result: 在铁磁区，加入双翻转更新可使多数味占据数的积分自相关时间最小；在顺磁区，加入味置换更新最有效。通过分析被接受更新引起的特征占据变化，阐明了二者差异的来源。

Conclusion: 不同更新方案对采样效率的改善依赖于磁有序相：铁磁区宜采用双翻转更新，顺磁区宜采用味置换更新，其效果源于更新导致的占据数变化特征差异。

Abstract: We investigate the sampling efficiency in a segment-based continuous-time hybridization-expansion quantum Monte Carlo solver within dynamical mean-field theory for the doped SU(3) Fermi-Hubbard model. Focusing on the low-temperature and strong-coupling regime, where a ferromagnetic state appears upon hole doping away from one-third filling, we compare several update schemes. Adding the double-flip update to the basic local update yields the smallest integrated autocorrelation time of the majority-flavor occupation in the ferromagnetic regime, whereas adding the flavor-permutation update is the most effective in the paramagnetic regime. We further clarify the origin of this difference by analyzing the characteristic occupation changes caused by accepted updates.

</details>


### [106] [Strong coupling polarons in cavity quantum materials: limits of cavity-induced electron mass renormalization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25829)
*D. M. Basko*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 该文研究腔诱导电子质量重整化的极限问题，指出其源于电子与腔镜中可极化自由度之间的场介导相互作用，并将其映射为平面几何中的Landau-Pekar极化子问题，通过数值精确量子蒙特卡罗方法直接计算二维电子的费曼路径积分，给出了该质量重整化的上限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 腔诱导电子质量重整化是通过真空场调控材料性质的重要机制，但其最终极限尚不明确。明确这一上限对于评估腔控制方案的可行性与理论边界至关重要。

Method: (i) 从物理机制上将质量重整化归因于场介导的电子与腔镜可极化自由度之间的相互作用；(ii) 将问题转化为平面几何下的Landau-Pekar极化子模型；(iii) 对二维电子的相应费曼路径积分进行数值精确的量子蒙特卡罗计算。

Result: 所寻求的质量重整化上限由平面几何中的Landau-Pekar极化子给出；数值精确的量子蒙特卡罗计算定量刻画了该上限，并连接了腔控制与介电工程两条调控材料性质的路径。

Conclusion: 腔诱导电子质量重整化存在由Landau-Pekar极化子设定的上限，该计算在腔控制与介电工程之间架起桥梁。

Abstract: Cavity-induced electron mass renormalization is an important ingredient of many proposals to control material properties through vacuum field modification. What is its ultimate limit? Here this question is answered by (i)~noting that this mass renormalization is essentially due to the field-mediated interaction between the electron and the polarizable degrees of freedom in the cavity mirrors, and (ii)~directly evaluating the corresponding Feynman path integral for a 2D electron by numerically exact quantum Monte Carlo. The sought upper limit is provided by the Landau-Pekar polaron in the planar geometry. The presented calculation bridges the gap between cavity control and dielectric engineering of material properties.

</details>


### [107] [Nullspace-guided Adaptive Bootstrap of Quantum Many-body Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25834)
*Xu-Cheng Wang,Yang Qi*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 提出零空间引导自适应（NGA）bootstrap方法，通过动态增量地精炼bootstrap基，显著提升量子多体基态能量下界精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子多体基态能量下界的bootstrap方法通常受限于固定或静态基，难以在强关联体系中达到高精度；亟需一种能自适应发现体系底层算符结构的可扩展方案。

Method: NGA在每次迭代中求解优化后的矩矩阵，其零空间对应饱和正定性方向，被解释为近似基态子空间的湮灭算符；据此剪除零空间杠杆小的算符，并沿零方向的“后代”扩展基，实现动态增量精炼。

Result: 在横场Ising链上得到近乎精确的能量下界，并自动发现Jordan-Wigner费米子形式的本征算符结构；在Hubbard链上，强关联区能量下界误差较最优结果改善最多两个数量级，达到10^{-3}至10^{-5}；同时NGA框架可用于改进一般可观测量的双侧认证界。

Conclusion: NGA bootstrap能够自适应发现底层算符结构，且误差随计算资源近似呈幂律下降，为一般量子多体体系的精确bootstrap提供了实用且可扩展的路径。

Abstract: We introduce a nullspace-guided adaptive (NGA) bootstrap method that improves the energy lower bounds of quantum many-body ground states by refining the bootstrap basis in a dynamic and incremental way. At each iteration, the optimized moment matrix reveals a nullspace of saturated positivity directions, which is intuitively interpreted as annihilators of the approximate ground-state subspace. The NGA bootstrap then prunes operators with small nullspace leverage and grows the basis along descendants of these null directions. By applying the NGA bootstrap to the transverse-field Ising chain, we obtain nearly exact energy lower bounds because the algorithm automatically discovers the eigenoperator structure in terms of Jordan-Wigner fermions from a minimal local bootstrap basis. For the Hubbard chain, it improves upon state-of-the-art energy lower bounds by up to two orders of magnitude, reaching errors ranging from $10^{-3}$ down to $10^{-5}$ in the strongly correlated regimes. We further show that the NGA framework can be used to improve the certified two-sided bounds on general observables. In addition, the bootstrap error decreases approximately as a power law with increasing computational resources. These results suggest that our method provides a practical and scalable route toward accurate bootstrap of general quantum many-body systems.

</details>


### [108] [Fractional Quantum Geometry in Topological Mott Regime](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25922)
*Junyu Tang,Hongquan Lv,Gang v. Chen*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文提出通过光学响应测量二维拓扑Mott绝缘体（手性自旋液体）中自旋子的量子几何性质。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 受电子能带量子几何研究启发，关注拓扑Mott绝缘体中分数化准粒子的量子几何，但其电荷中性导致难以直接探测。

Method: 考虑手性自旋液体，其自旋子能带具有非平凡拓扑与量子几何；有能隙的电荷扇区虚极化通过涌现U(1)规范场将电磁驱动传递给自旋子，产生张量Ioffe-Larkin响应。据此设计两种光学方案：低频（离壳）区利用纵向与霍尔电导比自校准提取自旋子Chern数；共振（在壳）区结合逆Ioffe-Larkin响应与Kramers-Kronig关系重构量子度规与Berry曲率谱密度。

Result: 在低频区无需电荷响应微观细节即可得到自旋子Chern数；共振区可重构量子度规和Berry曲率的谱密度。方法直接适用于Moiré体系中的三角晶格Hofstadter-Hubbard模型，并可推广至Nb3Br8等三角晶格反铁磁体。

Conclusion: 建立了定量探测拓扑Mott区分数化准粒子量子几何的统一框架。

Abstract: Inspired by recent interests in quantum geometry in electronic bands, we consider a two-dimensional topological Mott insulator, i.e. chiral spin liquid, whose spinon bands develop nontrivial band topological and quantum geometry. The virtual polarization of the gapped charge sector transfers the electromagnetic drive to the charge-neutral spinons through the emergent U(1) gauge field, producing a tensor Ioffe-Larkin response and enabling two complementary optical protocols for measurements. In the off-shell (low-frequency) regime, a self-calibrated ratio of the physical longitudinal and Hall conductivities yields the spinon Chern number without the microscopic knowledge of the charge response. In the on-shell (resonant) regime, combining the inverse Ioffe-Larkin response with the Kramers-Kronig relation enables reconstruction of the quantum-metric and Berry-curvature spectral densities. Our approach directly applies to the triangular lattice Hofstadter-Hubbard model in Moiré systems, can be well adapted to other triangular lattice antiferromagnets such as Nb$_3$Br$_8$ with appropriate modifications. We establish a unified framework for quantitatively probing the quantum geometry of fractionalized quasiparticles in topological Mott regime.

</details>


### [109] [Effect of interaction range on the phase diagram of magnetic hedgehog lattices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26373)
*Midori Yamada,Kotaro Shimizu,Shun Okumura,Yasuyuki Kato,Yukitoshi Motome*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文通过系统调节交换相互作用的范围与空间衰减，研究了三维拓扑自旋结构磁刺猬晶格（HLs）的稳定性，发现中等范围相互作用最有利于稳定HLs，并产生两种不同的3Q-HLs。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 拓扑自旋结构因拓扑霍尔效应和拓扑能斯特效应等涌现电磁性质而备受关注，其中磁刺猬晶格（HLs）是承载涌现磁单极子的三维拓扑自旋结构，已在MnSi1-xGex和SrFeO3等金属化合物中被发现。已有理论模型分别采用短程或长程相互作用来有效描述绝缘体和金属体系，但相互作用范围在稳定HLs中的作用仍只被部分理解，需要填补这一空白。

Method: 系统改变交换相互作用的范围与空间衰减，在局域自旋绝缘体模型与巡游电子金属模型之间进行插值，基于大规模蒙特卡洛模拟研究HLs的稳定性。

Result: HLs在广泛的相互作用范围内均被稳定。在所考察的各种相互作用分布中，中等范围相互作用特别有利于稳定HLs，并产生两种不同的3Q-HLs，它们的区别在于每个磁单胞中磁单极-反磁单极对的数量不同，且其相对稳定性对相互作用范围敏感。

Conclusion: 研究结果提供了从微观相互作用模型出发调控相图的统一框架，可作为解释候选材料实验的指导。

Abstract: Topological spin textures have attracted considerable attention through their emergent electromagnetic properties, including the topological Hall effect and the topological Nernst effect. Among them, magnetic hedgehog lattices (HLs) are three-dimensional topological spin textures that host emergent magnetic monopoles and antimonopoles. HLs have been identified in metallic compounds, such as $\mathrm{MnSi}_{1-x}\mathrm{Ge}_x$ and $\mathrm{SrFeO_3}$. Various theoretical models have been proposed, with either short-range or long-range interactions, which are typically regarded as effective descriptions of insulating and metallic systems, respectively. However, the role of the interaction range in stabilizing the HLs remains only partially understood. To address this gap, we investigate the stability of HLs by systematically varying the range and spatial decay of the exchange interactions, thereby interpolating between interaction profiles commonly associated with localized-spin insulating models and itinerant-electron metallic models. Based on extensive Monte Carlo simulations, we find that HLs are stabilized over a broad interaction range. Intriguingly, among the interaction profiles examined, intermediate-range interactions are particularly favorable for stabilizing HLs. They give rise to two distinct types of $3Q$-HLs that differ in the number of monopole-antimonopole pairs per magnetic unit cell, and their relative stability depends sensitively on the interaction range. Our results provide a unified framework for tuning phase diagrams from microscopic interaction models, serving as a guideline for interpreting experiments in candidate materials.

</details>


### [110] [Extraordinary Lifetime Enhancement of Coherent Phonon-Amplitude Modes in the Excitonic Insulator Phase of Ta2Pd3Te5](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26485)
*Anjan Kumar N M,Shuhan Wang,Snehashish Chatterjee,Yan Zhu,MinJae Kim,Tobias Ritschel,Elaheh Sadrollahi,Jochen Geck,Achim Rosch,Chandra Shekhar,Claudia Felser,Stefan Kaiser*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 该文研究无结构相变伴随的激子绝缘体Ta2Pd3Te5，通过相干声子振幅响应表征激子凝聚态，发现TC以下声子寿命异常增强，并将其确立为激子凝聚的新指纹。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多数原型激子绝缘体材料中同时存在结构相变，使得有序态的纯电子起源难以确认，因此需要一个无结构相变、能清晰区分电子驱动机制的体系及可靠判据。

Method: 以Ta2Pd3Te5为对象（TC约100 K以下出现激子绝缘相且无可探测结构相变），利用相干声子振幅响应表征激子凝聚态，并分析TC以下声子模式寿命的变化；结合激子与晶格耦合、序参量极化子图像及唯象模型进行解释。

Result: 观测到TC以下声子模式寿命出现异常强烈的增强，并将其确立为与激子凝聚相关联的稳健新指纹；该增强可由耦合声子振幅响应、序参量极化子图像（激子态耦合修饰声子模式、抑制弛豫）以及结合非谐声子响应和EI相能隙打开导致电子散射减小的唯象模型加以描述。

Conclusion: Ta2Pd3Te5中无结构相变下出现的激子绝缘相及声子寿命增强，为识别激子凝聚提供了新判据，并支持激子—晶格耦合框架下对相干声子响应的理解。

Abstract: The excitonic insulator (EI) is an electronic phase of condensed excitons. However, in many prototypical materials the presence of a concurrent structural transition complicates the identification of a purely electronic origin of the ordered state. Here, we investigate Ta2Pd3Te5, in which an EI phase develops below TC ~ 100 K in the absence of a detectable structural transition. We characterize the excitonic condensate via its coherent phonon-amplitude response. Most strikingly, an extraordinarily strong lifetime enhancement of these modes sets in below TC which we establish as a new and robust fingerprint linked to exciton condensation. We discuss possibilities to capture the enhancement when taking into account a coupling of excitonic and lattice effects in line with the coupled phonon-amplitude response. Within an order-parameter polaron picture coupling to the excitonic state may dress the phonon mode and thereby suppress its relaxation and a phenomenological model combines the anharmonic phonon response and a reduced electronic scattering due to the gap opening in the EI phase.

</details>


### [111] [Neural Network Backflow with Low-Rank Multi-Determinant Updates](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26544)
*Tianshu Huang,Xiaowei Ou,Vidvuds Ozolins*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 该论文提出一种以神经网络backflow变换为核心的变分框架，将深度学习与变分蒙特卡洛结合，用于模拟强关联费米子系统。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 强关联费米子模拟因希尔伯特空间指数复杂度和多体波函数的复杂符号结构而长期具有挑战性。

Method: 采用结合深度学习的神经网络backflow变换变分框架，并基于变分蒙特卡洛；所提议的拟设使用带低秩位移的多行列式展开，以捕捉非局域关联和复杂符号结构。

Result: 在二维Hubbard模型上，半填充时能量与辅助场量子蒙特卡洛相差在0.45%以内，并在1/8掺杂时捕捉到交织的电荷与自旋密度条纹图样。

Conclusion: 结果表明该框架可作为强关联费米子系统变分模拟的一种可扩展且可解释的方法。

Abstract: Simulating strongly correlated fermions remains a long-standing challenge due to the exponential complexity of the Hilbert space and the intricate sign structure of many-body wavefunctions. We introduce a variational framework centered on a neural network backflow transformation that combines deep learning with variational Monte Carlo. The proposed ansatz employs a multi-determinant expansion with low-rank shifts to capture non-local correlations and complex sign structures. Applied to the two-dimensional Hubbard model at both half-filling and $1/8$ doping, the method achieves energies within $0.45\%$ of auxiliary-field quantum Monte Carlo at half-filling and captures intertwined charge- and spin-density stripe patterns at $1/8$ doping. These results demonstrate the potential of this framework as a scalable and interpretable approach to variational simulations of strongly correlated fermionic systems.

</details>


### [112] [Crystal Electric Field Analysis on the Magnetic Properties of Ferromagnetic CeRu$_2$Ge$_2$ Single Crystal](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26587)
*Shovan Dan,Suman Nandi,Gourav Dwari,Bishal Baran Maity,Bhagyashree A Chalke,Ruta Kulkarni,P. D. Babu,Arumugam Thamizhavel*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文报道了CeRu₂Ge₂单晶磁性与输运性质的各向异性研究，确认了7.5 K的铁磁转变、磁振子激发能隙及晶体电场（CEF）能级结构，并发现其易磁化轴与CEF基态波函数一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 揭示CeRu₂Ge₂的各向异性磁性与输运行为，并通过晶体电场（CEF）框架解释磁化易轴，检验其与多数Ce基Kondo晶格铁磁体易轴偏离CEF基态的差异。

Method: 对CeRu₂Ge₂单晶开展磁化率、磁化强度、电阻率、磁阻（MR）及热容的详细各向异性测量；采用点电荷模型结合CEF框架对磁化率与磁化强度进行定量分析。

Result: 在T_C=7.5 K观察到明确铁磁转变；低温磁有序态内MR为正；在电阻率、MR和低温热容中确认存在磁振子激发能隙；[100]与[001]方向磁化率、磁化强度呈显著各向异性，可由CEF定量解释；CEF能级包含三个二重态，基态及491 K、671 K两个激发态，并由磁热容的Schottky效应进一步证实。

Conclusion: CeRu₂Ge₂的磁化行为与CEF导出的基态波函数完全一致，易磁化轴沿[001]方向，区别于多数易轴偏离CEF基态的Ce基Kondo晶格铁磁体。

Abstract: We report a detailed anisotropic studies on the magnetic and transport properties of a CeRu$_2$Ge$_2$ single crystal. A clear ferromagnetic transition is observed at $T_{\rm C} = 7.5$~K in the magnetic susceptibility, electrical resistivity and heat capacity measurements. The magnetoresistance (MR) is positive at low temperature within the magnetically ordered state. We confirmed the presence of a magnon excitation gap in the resistivity, MR and the low temperature heat capacity data. The pronounced anisotropy observed in the magnetic susceptibility and magnetization along the [100] and [001] directions is quantitatively explained within the crystal electric field (CEF) framework using the point-charge model. The analysis reveals a CEF level scheme comprising three doublets, with a ground state and two excited states at 491 and 671~K, that was further confirmed from the Schottky effect in the magnetic part of the heat capacity data. Unlike most Kondo lattice ferromagnets of Ce compounds, where the magnetic easy axis deviates from the expected CEF ground state, CeRu$_2$Ge$_2$ exhibits magnetization, which is fully consistent with the CEF derived ground state wave function, with the easy axis of magnetization aligned along the [001] direction.

</details>


### [113] [Berry-Landau Fermi-liquid theory: transport in presence of quantum geometry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26632)
*Shuai A. Chen,Roderich Moessner*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文建立了Berry-Landau费米液体理论，将量子几何（贝里相位与量子度规）作为低能基本要素，统一了准粒子、朗道相互作用与量子几何，并推导出其输运后果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统朗道费米液体理论无法将非平凡量子几何（贝里相位、量子度规）作为基本要素纳入描述，如何把量子几何与准粒子残余相互作用统一起来是一个挑战。

Method: 对跨越费米面的孤立能带中的无自旋费米子，通过Nozières–Luttinger构造推导朗道泛函；利用布洛赫波对微扰的瞬时响应，得到作用量中的反常贝里联络势和相互作用诱导的量子几何准粒子电流贡献；再借助电磁Ward恒等式获得守恒的物理电荷电流。

Result: 守恒电流为量子几何电流与裸漂移电流的组合；内禀反常霍尔电导由缀饰准粒子能带的贝里曲率积分决定，而Drude权重同时包含常规与量子几何贡献。存在伽利略对称性时Drude权重不受相互作用重整化；在平带/窄带极限，输运以量子几何为主并可被热增强。

Conclusion: 准粒子占据、朗道相互作用以及准粒子携带的量子几何共同构成Berry-Landau费米液体的基本低能要素。

Abstract: Landau Fermi-liquid theory characterizes interacting metals through quasiparticles and their residual interactions. It is a challenge to incorporate non-trivial quantum geometry -- as encoded by Berry phases and the quantum metric -- as a fundamental ingredient. We formulate a Berry-Landau Fermi-liquid theory for spinless fermions within an isolated band crossing the Fermi surface and derive the Landau functional through the Nozières--Luttinger construction. The instantaneous response to a perturbation of the Bloch waves generates both an anomalous Berry-connection potential in the action and an interaction-induced quantum-geometric contribution to the quasiparticle current. The conserved physical charge current is then obtained via the electromagnetic Ward identity as a combinati of this quantum-geometric current and the bare drift current. Therefore, the intrinsic anomalous Hall conductivity is fixed by the Berry-curvature integral of the dressed quasiparticle band while the Drude weight contains both conventional and quantum-geometric contributions. In the presence of Galilean symmetry, the Drude weight is protected against interaction renormalization. In the flat/narrow-band limit, transport is dominantly quantum-geometric and can be thermally enhanced. These results establish quasiparticle occupations, Landau interactions, and the quantum geometry carried by quasiparticles as the fundamental low-energy ingredients of a Berry-Landau Fermi liquid.

</details>


### [114] [Tilt-controlled Drude anisotropy in nodal-line semimetals: logarithmic enhancement, interband correction, gauge consistency, and the Landau-damping window](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26639)
*Wei Li,Jing-Rong Wang*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 研究三维倾斜节线半金属的各向异性电荷响应，用Kubo公式揭示倾斜诱导的Drude响应各向异性、等离激元色散及声学支。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 倾斜节线半金属的低能相空间因倾斜进入身份项而被变形，纵向与横向电荷响应是否产生参数上不同的标度行为、等离激元是否出现无阻尼窗口或新分支，尚缺乏系统解析刻画。

Method: 采用含线积分顶点的Kubo公式，将倾斜作为模型控制参数，解析推导纵向与横向Drude权重、带头阶√δ修正；计算带间极化率的长波q^2结构与红外可积性；求解等离激元色散的自洽方程并作带隙截止（类布里渊区）正规化。

Result: 纵向Drude权重随δ对数增长 D_zz=B ln(1/δ)，系数B=g/(2π)^2（在费米面尾位于紫外截断内时；固定物理截断下则饱和为截断决定的平台）；横向权重 D_⊥~δ^{-1}，标度更强；两者领头√δ修正解析获得。带间极化率保留q^2长波结构，红外通过费米液体Pauli阻塞可积但含缓慢紫外对数，乘性重整化集体模式，设定吸收阈值 ω_c=2μ/(1+|η_z|)。自洽解给出倾斜依赖的无阻尼窗口，边界为带间连续谱；对于η_z≳1.2，在有限动量转移窗口内出现粒子-空穴连续谱之外的候选低频声学支。

Conclusion: 倾斜显著改变节线半金属的纵向/横向电荷响应标度与等离激元谱结构，预测了type-II扩展下偏离粒子-空穴连续谱的低频声学类分支。

Abstract: We study the anisotropic charge response of a three-dimensional tilted nodal-line semimetal in which the tilt enters the spectrum through an identity term and acts as the model control parameter. Using the Kubo formula with line-integral vertices, we show that the tilt-induced deformation of the low-energy phase space produces parametrically different longitudinal and transverse Drude responses: the longitudinal weight grows logarithmically as $D_{zz}=B\ln(1/δ)$ with the analytically determined coefficient $B=g/(2π)^2$---within the window in which the Fermi-surface tail lies inside the ultraviolet cutoff, while at a fixed physical cutoff the weight saturates instead at a cutoff-set plateau---whereas the transverse weight follows a stronger algebraic scaling $D_\perp\simδ^{-1}$; the leading $\sqrtδ$ corrections to both asymptotic forms are obtained analytically. The interband polarization retains the $q^2$ long-wavelength structure, with a coefficient that is infrared-integrable through Pauli blocking at finite doping yet carries a slow ultraviolet logarithm; it renormalizes the collective mode multiplicatively and sets the interband absorption threshold $ω_c=2μ/(1+|η_z|)$. A self-consistent solution of the plasmon dispersion identifies a tilt-dependent damping-free window bounded by the interband continuum. We predict that the type-II extension of this window, within the stated bandwidth (Brillouin-zone-like) cutoff regularization, contains a candidate low-frequency acoustic-like branch outside the particle--hole continua for $η_z\gtrsim1.2$ over a finite window in momentum transfer.

</details>


### [115] [Real-Frequency Diagrammatics with Pole Representations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26703)
*Rayan Farid,Lei Zhang,Aiman Al-Eryani,Agnieszka Jażdżewska,Emanuel Gull*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文提出了基于极点和矩展开的可系统改进的框架，用于在实轴上直接计算多体微扰理论的谱性质，避免数值解析延拓。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 有限温多体理论通常在虚时间中表述，便于高效数值处理，但从虚轴提取谱性质需要数值解析延拓，精度和可控性差；而直接在实轴上表述则需极其昂贵的数值求积，因此需要一种兼顾效率与直接性的实轴计算方法。

Method: 将多体微扰理论中的对象表示为可系统改进的极点与矩展开，构建直接在实轴上评估图形（图）贡献的框架，并引入受控的重压缩（recompression）程序，以抑制代数运算和图形操作中极点数目的增殖。

Result: 在典型量子杂质问题上进行了基准测试，并应用于均匀电子气的自洽计算，所得谱量随展开阶数系统收敛至数值精度。

Conclusion: 该工作为图形多体理论中可控且可系统改进的谱量计算提供了实用框架，包括自洽守恒方法，避免了实轴求积的高昂代价和解析延拓的不稳定性。

Abstract: Finite-temperature many-body theories are typically formulated in imaginary time, where they are amenable to efficient numerical treatment. However, the extraction of spectral properties from the imaginary axis is difficult and requires numerical analytic continuation, while a direct formulation on the real axis requires prohibitively expensive numerical quadrature. In this paper, we represent the objects of many-body perturbation theory using systematically improvable pole and moment expansions and develop the corresponding framework for evaluating diagrammatic contributions directly on the real axis. We introduce a controlled recompression procedure that prevents the proliferation of poles under algebraic and diagrammatic operations. We benchmark the approach on paradigmatic quantum impurity problems and apply it to self-consistent calculations of the uniform electron gas. The resulting spectral quantities converge systematically towards numerical accuracy. This work provides a practical framework for controlled and systematically improvable calculations of spectral quantities within diagrammatic many-body theories, including self-consistent conserving approaches.

</details>


<div id='cond-mat.dis-nn'></div>

# cond-mat.dis-nn [[Back]](#toc)

### [116] [Disorder-promoted stability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25226)
*Arthur N. Montanari,Pietro Zanin,Adilson E. Motter*

Main category: cond-mat.dis-nn

TL;DR: 该文指出，以往"节点异质性抑制网络稳定性"的结论源于为数学可解性所做的模型简化；当节点动力学为高维、雅可比矩阵为非厄米时，异质性反而能增强稳定性，非厄米性也是网络异质性稳定效应的根源，甚至在一维节点动力学中通过非互易相互作用即可出现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 既有网络动力学研究普遍认为节点异质性会抑制稳定性，这与自然和工程系统中普遍存在的内在异质性现象相矛盾。作者旨在解释这一矛盾，并重新审视异质性与稳定性的关系。

Method: 通过放宽为追求数学可解性而引入的模型简化，考虑节点动力学为高维、对应非厄米雅可比矩阵的情形；并结合神经网络、电网、材料网络以及生态网络（含非互易相互作用）等案例进行分析。

Result: 在非厄米系统中，节点异质性可增强稳定性，即使参数为随机无序亦然；非厄米性同样是网络异质性稳定效应的基础，且该效应在一维节点动力学中经由非互易相互作用即可出现。

Conclusion: 无序并非系统的负担，而是可用于稳定复杂系统的一种普遍资源。

Abstract: Previous studies of network dynamics have suggested that heterogeneity among nodes inhibits stability, at odds with the ubiquity of inherently heterogeneous natural and engineered systems. Here, we show that this conclusion arises from model reductions introduced for mathematical tractability and breaks down when nodal dynamics are higher-dimensional, yielding non-Hermitian Jacobians. In such systems, including neural, power-grid, and material networks, nodal heterogeneity can instead enhance stability, even when parameters are randomly disordered. Non-Hermiticity also underlies the stabilizing effects of network heterogeneity, which can arise even in one-dimensional nodal dynamics through nonreciprocal interactions, as shown for ecological networks. Our framework reveals disorder not as a liability but as a general resource for stabilizing complex systems.

</details>


### [117] [Measure-zero delocalization in the complex plane: exact mobility arcs in a non-Hermitian off-diagonal quasiperiodic lattice](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25877)
*Fu-Xi Zhong,Yi-Cai Zhang*

Main category: cond-mat.dis-nn

TL;DR: 本文将非厄米非对角准周期无序引入一维格点，推广Hermitian mosaic模型，用Avila全局理论解析求出Lyapunov指数与复能平面完整相图，发现"迁移弧"与迁移环互补共存，并揭示二者同源于广义Joukowski变换P(E)=½(u−w²/u)。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有非厄米Anderson局域化中迁移边多为环状有限区域或线状零测集，缺乏对更一般开迁移边几何形态及其代数起源的统一刻画；作者希望把近期基于Hermitian mosaic模型的结论拓展到非厄米情形，厘清复平面上离域集合的普遍结构。

Method: 构建一维带非厄米非对角准周期无序的格点模型；运用Avila全局理论计算精确Lyapunov指数；通过多项式映射的代数结构分析复能平面相图，重点考察广义Joukowski变换P(E)=½(u−w²/u)的像与原像。

Result: 导出精确Lyapunov指数和复能平面完整相图；发现迁移弧（复平面中开放曲线段）作为新型迁移边，与迁移环在互补参数区间共存；离域态仅存在于零测度的弧或线上（相对环所围有限面积区域），呈现测度零离域现象；证明环、弧、线均统一源于P(E)映射：椭圆区域边界在P(E)下的原像给出环，椭圆内部支割线给出互补参数区的弧与线。

Conclusion: 非厄米准周期体系中开迁移边（弧）是普适几何形态，与闭迁移环互补共存，且二者及迁移线共享同一代数起源，统一了复能平面中各类迁移边的刻画。

Abstract: We investigate Anderson localization in a one-dimensional lattice with non-Hermitian off-diagonal quasiperiodic disorder, extending a recently studied Hermitian mosaic model to the non-Hermitian regime. Using Avila's global theory, we derive the exact Lyapunov exponent and the complete phase diagram in the complex energy plane. This work contains two central findings. First, we discover mobility arcs---open curved segments in the complex plane---as a new class of mobility edges and the generic form of open mobility edges, which coexist with closed mobility rings in a complementary parameter regime. These arcs share the same localization physics as the previously reported mobility lines: eigenstates are delocalized if and only if their energies lie exactly on these sets; any deviation yields localized states. This constitutes a striking measure-zero delocalization phenomenon: delocalized states occupy only zero-measure sets (arcs or lines) in the complex plane, in sharp contrast to the mobility rings, which enclose a finite-area region of delocalized states. Second, we reveal that mobility rings, arcs, and lines all share a common mathematical origin in the generalized Joukowski transformation $P(E) = \frac{1}{2}(u - w^2/u)$, rooted in the algebraic structure of the underlying polynomial: the preimage of the boundary of an elliptical region under the polynomial map $P(E)$ gives the rings, while the branch cut inside this ellipse gives rise to the mobility arcs and lines in the complementary parameter regime.

</details>


### [118] [Topological phase transition driven by structural defects](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26586)
*Andrii Syrota,Andrej Mesaros,Pascal Simon*

Main category: cond-mat.dis-nn

TL;DR: 本文通过连续引入Stone-Wales缺陷，将装饰蜂窝晶格从晶态驱动到超均匀非晶态，并利用拓扑Weaire-Thorpe模型研究其电子性质，发现SW缺陷可导致赝能带反转和Chern数反号，进而预测了非晶态的拓扑相图。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 结构缺陷（如向错和位错）的增殖会破坏长程晶体序，但缺陷如何影响系统的拓扑性质尚需深入理解。本文旨在探究从晶态到非晶态的连续转变过程中，Stone-Wales缺陷对拓扑相的影响机制。

Method: 采用装饰蜂窝晶格模型，连续引入Stone-Wales缺陷（可视为位错偶极子）驱动系统从晶态向超均匀非晶态转变；使用拓扑Weaire-Thorpe哈密顿量描述电子；结合绝热论证和计算高效的spillage指标预测拓扑相图；并通过基于单个SW缺陷的有效哈密顿量中的跃迁重整化解释赝能带反转的本质。

Result: Stone-Wales缺陷可引起赝能带反转，并相应地导致Chern数反号；利用绝热论证和spillage指标成功从晶态预测了非晶态的拓扑相图；通过有效哈密顿量中跃迁的重整化阐明了赝能带反转的物理本质。

Conclusion: Stone-Wales缺陷作为一种位错偶极子，能够通过诱导赝能带反转和Chern数反号来调控系统的拓扑相，从而在从晶态到非晶态的连续转变中实现拓扑相变，为理解和预测非晶态拓扑性质提供了有效方法。

Abstract: Structural defects, such as disclinations and dislocations, destroy the long-range crystalline order as they proliferate. In this work, we continuously drive a system from a crystalline state on a decorated honeycomb lattice to a hyperuniform amorphous state, by consecutively introducing Stone-Wales (SW) defects, which can be viewed as dipoles of dislocations. Using a topological Weaire-Thorpe Hamiltonian model to describe electrons in this system, we demonstrate that SW defects can cause pseudo-band inversions and, correspondingly, a reversal of the Chern number. Using adiabatic arguments and a computationally efficient spillage indicator, we predict the topological phase diagram of the amorphous state from the crystalline one. We explain the nature of the pseudo-band inversion through the renormalization of the hopping in an effective Hamiltonian based on a single SW defect.
